The PNG is encoded to a `BytesIO` and written via `atomic_io.atomic_write_bytes` so a power cut mid-save can't leave a truncated frame for the next tick (and for `display_inky.py`) to read. The underlying PIL `Image` is explicitly `close()`d after encoding to release the file handle — important over months of continuous operation.
- **Hot-path caches.** `fit_quote` calls `load_font` up to 18 times per render with the same candidate chain at different sizes; without memoisation that's 36 path-existence scans + 36 `ImageFont.truetype` opens per render.

    **`_FONT_CACHE`** keys on `(normalised_candidates, size)` so repeats are O(1), and the variation tuple form `(path, "Bold")` keys distinctly from the plain string so a per-theme variable-font Bold/Regular split stays isolated. **The bitmap fallback is deliberately NOT cached** — a transient miss (NFS hiccup, brief filesystem unavailability) would otherwise pin the subprocess to degraded rendering for its lifetime, and `contact_sheet.py` renders 144 frames in one process. The cache's lifetime is the renderer subprocess (single-threaded), so no FD-leak risk; `tests/test_render_quote.py` clears it autouse around every test so cache state from a prior test can't mask path-existence assertions in the current one.

    **Regexes and widths.** Time-phrase regex compilation lives at module load (`_TIME_PHRASE_PATTERNS`, sorted longest-first), and `_direct_match_pattern` shares one compiled pattern between `resolve_display_match` and `tokenize_quote` instead of recompiling per call. `wrap_styled_text` memoises space widths per font-id within a single call (a 140-char quote has ~25 spaces × 18 fit iterations × 2 fonts → ~450 redundant `textbbox` calls per render without the memo).

## Synthesising colours outside the Spectra 6 palette

See [`spectra6_color_recipes.md`](spectra6_color_recipes.md) for the full recipe catalogue (in-use + reachable-but-unused), calibrated panel-ink values, the octahedron colour model, and the step-by-step authoring playbook. The table below is the short in-use-only reference for code readers; the linked doc is the forward-looking reference for theme authors.

Spectra 6 only has six inks (white / black / red / yellow / blue / green). Any other colour a theme wants — orange, mint, purple, sky blue, brown — must be **synthesised in software** by interleaving two natives on a stipple pattern. The PNG stays fully on-palette so `snap_image_to_palette` and the panel's own internal dithering pass don't re-quantise it into something else, and the eye averages adjacent pixels into the perceived mixed colour at panel-viewing distance (1–3 m for the Idle Hours appliance).

**Principle.** Decompose the target colour into a weighted mix of the nearest base inks, then paint each pixel as exactly one base ink chosen so the local density matches the weights. Reference framework: [Frans-Willem/epd-dither](https://github.com/Frans-Willem/epd-dither) treats RGB as an octahedron with the six inks at the vertices and any interior point as a barycentric mix. Confirmation in the Spectra 6 community ("Beyond 6 Colors" / `epdoptimize` / Pimoroni forum): the same approach extends the visible palette to ~12–13 usable colours total.

**Caveat:** the panel's measured-ink RGB drifts per unit (calibrated values from `Utzel-Butzel/epdoptimize`: panel red ≈ `#62201E`, yellow ≈ `#C1BB1E`, blue ≈ `#233F8E`, green ≈ `#35563A`, black ≈ `#1F2226`, white ≈ `#B9C7C9`), so the recipes below are **textbook starting points**, not pixel-perfect specs — bias toward the more-saturated ink if a result reads washed-out at panel distance.

**Patterns.** `draw_text_dithered` already supports all three branches and `BAYER_4x4` is the shared 4×4 ordered matrix for the third. For ratios above 50%, swap `dark`/`light` and pass the complementary density (e.g. "5/8 yellow + 3/8 red" = `dark=yellow, light=red, light_density=0.375`).

| Pattern | "Light" density | Trigger | Visual signature |
|---|---|---|---|
| Sparse 1-in-4 (`x%2 == 0 and y%2 == 0`) | 25% | `light_density <= 0.25` | Subtle wash of "light" over dominant "dark" |
| 4×4 ordered Bayer (`BAYER_4x4[y%4][x%4] < round(d*16)`) | any `d ∈ (0.25, 0.5)` | `0.25 < light_density < 0.5` | Biased mix dispersed across a 4×4 tile |
| 1×1 checkerboard (`(x+y) & 1`) | 50% | `light_density >= 0.5` | Equal-weight midpoint of two inks |

**Two-ink recipes** (named tones the literature consistently cites; **In use** column flags which themes already pull each recipe today).

| Synthesised colour | Recipe | Pattern call | In use |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tangerine / warm orange | red + yellow at 5/8 : 3/8 | `dark=red, light=yellow, light_density=0.375` | `deco` (body matched phrase + border post-pass); `atomic` (mid-edge starburst rays — bbox-scoped post-pass flips ~3/8 of the painted red to yellow per Bayer threshold 6/16; atom-symbol orbits stay solid red for ray-vs-orbit contrast); `alchemy` (🜂 Fire element triangle at the BL of the transmutation circle); `lcars` (top + bottom horizontal bars + elbows — bbox post-pass over the bar/elbow rectangles flips ~3/8 of the painted red to yellow, plus the second rail block from the top); `astrarium` (matched phrase + quote-mark ornaments via `_astrarium_paint_quote_panel`); `vitrail` (amber/gold glass pane); `nocturne` (every gold bloom — shore lights, reflections, rocket sparks, matched phrase — the bakelite split-band glow reused via `_nocturne_paint_gold`); `trisolaris` (the sun blooms' halos, the cooling orbit wakes, the Radar Peak crest and the dish's wavefronts — every warm line, because solid red barely clears the panel's black); `carcosa` (the fold shadows on the tattered curtains — each crease laid as a red shadow, then Bayer-flipped 3/8 to yellow); `observation` (Saturn's belts and the matched phrase's halo — the belts through a single-read three-way partition with the terminator's black, the halo through the `bakelite` split-band glow); `betweenus` (the "Like it" legend dot — the app's warm terracotta — and the leading edge of the day-progress fill, whose yellow share falls from gold at the left to tangerine at the front); `codex` (a stem band, the fish-leaf tails, the inner sepal ring); `bosch` (Hell's fire — the blaze blooms a tangerine glow into the black above the burning city through `paint_neon_mask`'s split glow, red major with a 3/8 yellow minor); `saros` (the matched phrase's ember bloom — the same split glow — and, in continuous tone rather than by recipe, the whole corona: the mid term is painted `(255, 112, 0)` and Floyd–Steinberg lands it on this pair at a ratio that follows the falloff); `atropos` (the matched phrase — a yellow core in the split halo on black); `expedition` (the painted hour and the matched phrase — a yellow core with white at 1/4 in a red + yellow halo at **1/2** rather than 3/8, the `atropos` scene-light argument; the number's reflection on the water is the same two inks as a density stipple); `witcher` (matched phrase — the interface's red-orange on cream parchment); `splitflap` (the orange colour tile) |
| Pure orange / amber | red + yellow at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=red, light=yellow` (default density) | `gothic` (matched phrase — warm candle-flame against the black cathedral ground, and it lifts the phrase clear of the red border ornaments rather than sharing their ink). Was also `deco`'s recipe before it moved to the red-biased tangerine above: 50/50 reads as washed-out amber because yellow out-luminates red, which is the failure there and the point here; `betweenus_dark` (matched phrase — the yellow sentinel rerouted in `_draw_text_body`; the apricot the app's dark `want` #DFA07C becomes on these inks, and the "Like it" legend dot); `furies` (the cadmium-orange ground of all three panels — Floyd–Steinberg-separated against red+yellow only, so the mix floats near even with the scumble rather than sitting on a fixed ratio); `expanse` (the MCRN orange — matched phrase and sender via `draw_text_dithered`; header leads, lit pills, inner gauge sweeps, the gas giant, the contact's list glyph and the cursor via `_fill_swatch_stipple`; on black glass the red-biased tangerine goes dim, so this is the even mix) |
| Amber sand (yellow-biased orange) | yellow + red at 3/4 : 1/4 | a 1-in-4 red lattice pasted over the yellow mask (the `light_density <= 0.25` sparse pattern, by hand) | `hades` (the hourglass's sand — on its own the panel's yellow reads lemon; a red quarter warms it to amber without the tangerine's weight, which would read as rust at 20 px) |
| Yellow ochre / raw umber | yellow + black + red (+ white) by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a continuous-tone field against the calibrated inks — roughly Y 1/2 : K 1/4 : R 1/8 : W 1/8 for the pale passage, K 3/4 : Y 1/8 : R 1/8 for the umber | `goya` (the void and the slope of *El Perro* — the panel's yellow alone is lemon; black pulls it to ochre, a red eighth warms it to earth, and a white eighth lifts the passage the quote sits in) |
| Beige terminal housing | white + yellow at 3/4 : 1/4 | `_fill_swatch_stipple(dark=white, light=yellow, 0.25)` | `lumon` (the case around the glass — the panel's white alone is clinical, a yellow quarter is the show's tired cream plastic); `yorha` (the whole ground — Automata's parchment cream, under its dot grid) |
| Desert haze (orange in depth) | red + yellow + black + white by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a continuous-tone field whose every stop is a blend of the measured inks — about R 5/8 : Y 3/8 for the haze, R 1/2 : Y 2/5 : W 1/10 at the horizon, R 7/10 : K 1/5 : Y 1/10 for the far towers | `lasvegas` (the dead Las Vegas — the panel's red and yellow carry the orange with nothing synthesised, black and white give it depth and a sun) |
| Light-table glass (grey with a white pool) | white + black by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a continuous-tone field — the panel's white at the pool, roughly W 13/14 : K 1/14 for the glass, W 7/8 : K 1/8 for a frosted pane | `oblivion` (the Sky Tower's desk — the light comes *through* the glass, and a flat white never reads as lit); `dsky` (the Apollo console's grey, about W 1/2 : K 1/2 with a brushed grain, and the unit's rim, caps and screws modelled in the same two inks) |
| Parchment cream with shadows | white + yellow + black by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a cream field (W 3/4 : Y 1/4) with black only where the vignette, the city, the hatch and the panels' shadows darken it | `yorha` (the sheet behind Automata's menu; the panels' own faces are laid crisp afterwards as a Bayer W 7/8 : Y 1/8) |
| X-ray (bone on a light box) | black + blue + white by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a luminance field mapped onto a black → blue → white ramp — black to the bottom tenth, marrow about B 3/4 : W 1/4, the cortical rims white | `bladerunner` (the box's pelvis on the LAPD scan — blue alone is too dark for bone; the white rims give it its edge) |
| CRT vignette (blue falling to black) | blue + black by error diffusion | `_dither_calibrated` of a continuous-tone radial field against the calibrated inks — pure blue at the centre, roughly B 1/2 : K 1/2 at the corners | `lumon` (the Macrodata Refinement screen — the panel's blue is the screen's phosphor as it is; the fall-off is what makes a flat rectangle read as a tube) |
| Sodium night sky | black + blue + red by error diffusion, with grain | `_dither_calibrated` of a sky ramp (K at the zenith to about K 3/5 : B 2/5 at the horizon) under a soft R+K skyglow band, with ±12 levels of `_white_noise` grain added first, because Floyd–Steinberg over a smooth ramp settles into a regular dot lattice | `gantry` (the sky and the asphalt) |
| Warm-white bloom | white + yellow at about 2/3 : 1/3, falling density | `paint_neon_mask(glow=white, glow_minor=yellow, glow_minor_share=0.35, tile=BAYER_8x8)` | `gantry` (the matched phrase's white LEDs: a yellow bloom in the 1 px gaps of the doubled-column bold speckled the letters from inside) |
| Pink / coral | red + white at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=red, light=white` | `placard` (thumbtack corner-accent post-pass — weathered hand-painted red); `chalkboard` (eraser-smudge dots along the bottom inner edge — leftover pink eraser-stub residue); `lcars` (third rail block from the top — bbox post-pass flips half the painted red pixels to white on the (x+y)&1 checkerboard); `vitrail` (rose/coral glass pane); `betweenus_dark` (the "Love it" legend dot — the app lightens its deep terracotta to salmon in dark); `codex` (alternate petals of the eye-flower); `expedition` (the Gommage petals — teardrop polygons filled with the 1/2 stipple, a white rim toward the light and a red one away from it; the only way a petal reads against a blue night when the panel's red is as dark as it is) |
| Candlelit red | red + white at 3/4 : 1/4 | `dark=red, light=white, light_density=0.25` | `gantry` (the tail-light trails' cores, through `paint_neon_mask`'s `core_minor=white, core_minor_share=0.25`: a thin streak burning in a red bloom reads hot where a broad text fill read dim). `gothic` has moved off it as a text fill: its matched phrase is the R+Y amber above, because at panel distance a 3/4-red mix on a black ground read as dim rather than candlelit) |
| Mint | green + white at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=green, light=white` | `nightvision` (body / attribution / ornament); `marker` (left mid-edge dot — highlighter wash); `vitrail` (mint glass pane); `codex` (fish-leaf bodies) |
| Purple / violet | red + blue at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=red, light=blue` | `alchemy` (matched phrase; 🜁 Air element triangle); `marker` (right mid-edge dot — "second marker dragged over first" overlap); `tarot` (card-name chrome — the trump's own name in Tyrian purple along the foot of the rubricated vellum card); `vitrail` (matched phrase — violet glass — plus a royal-purple glass pane); `cardcatalog` (date-due stamps + matched phrase — library date stamps really were purple); `codex` (a stem band, alternate petals, the rainbow's puddle); `atropos` (the violet orbs' halo — a white core in a blue + red split bloom through `paint_neon_mask`); `splitflap` (the violet colour tile) |
| Sky blue | blue + white at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=blue, light=white` | `alchemy` (🜄 Water element triangle at the TR of the transmutation circle); `vitrail` (sky-blue glass pane); `expanse` (arc-gauge sweeps, the waveform's area and the signal bars, via `_fill_swatch_stipple` through an `L` mask — the console's cyan, on black glass) |
| Cyan | green + blue at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=green, light=blue` | — |
| Teal (green-biased cyan) | green + blue at 5/8 : 3/8 | `dark=green, light=blue, light_density=0.375` | `vitrail` (teal glass pane); `codex` (fish-leaf bodies); `atropos` (the whole night — continuous-tone fog FS-dithered to K/B/G/W, so the ratio falls with the fog rather than holding at 5/8) |
| Brown / sepia | red + green at 1/2 : 1/2 (mute further with black) | `dark=red, light=green`; `daguerreotype` (tarnish ring creeping in from the oval mat window — parity-gated, density rising toward the rim so it doubles as the vignette) | `saloon` (foxing speckles via `(px+py)&1` parity over `_SALOON_FOXING` reads as rust-brown aged-paper foxing; outer 3 px wanted-poster frame painted in red then its 4 edge strips post-passed to flip half to green per parity so the rule reads as rusted iron); `placard` (outer sign-painter's frame — perimeter post-pass flips half of the red 1 px rule's pixels to green so the rule reads as weathered sandwich-board wood); `dispatch` (alternating tractor-feed perforations — every other pair painted as red sentinel circles then bbox-post-passed for "carbon-paper bleed" oxidation); `newsprint` (Layer 0 foxing speckles — 1 red + 1 green pixel per 4×4 Bayer tile at cell values 2 and 3, blends at panel distance into pale lignin-oxidation rust-brown alongside the existing 12.5% black halftone); `cardcatalog` (manila card ground — the same cream-under-foxing pairing at ~1/5 the density; the foxing lattice must stay coprime with the cream wash's Bayer period or the wash silently cancels it, see the theme paragraph); `tarot` (Layer 0 foxing-stipple via `_tarot_paint_vellum` — 3.5% R+G dots from a seeded `randbytes` field over a Y+W cream base wash, the layered aged-vellum recipe distinguishing the cream-with-foxing tarot card stock from the clean cream of `astrarium`); `betweenus` (the 1 px card and pill outline — the app's `line` token on the light paper); `codex` (the specimen's spiral roots — drawn into a mask and filled through the recipe, no red sentinel) |
| Gray / pewter | black + white at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=black, light=white` | `semiotic` (the grey fields of Cobb's "system" signs — laser, bridge, computer terminal — phased on absolute canvas coordinates); `biomech` (the whole Giger wall — continuous tone from `shade_height_field`, FS-dithered to K+W, so every grey from shadow to glint is this pair at a varying ratio); `daguerreotype` (the case rim around the brass mat — the recipes doc's long-open "engineering monochrome" forward reference, claimed); `betweenus` / `betweenus_dark` (the "Neutral" legend dot — the app's stone) |
| Dark green / forest | green + black at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=green, light=black` | `vitrail` (forest "deep glass" pane); `circuit` (Layer-0 PCB soldermask wash — flips half the flat-green ground to black so the board reads as deep bottle-green FR-4) |
| Olive | yellow + green at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=yellow, light=green` | `roman` (laurel-sprig leaves on the corona triumphalis — bbox-post-pass for canonical Mediterranean laurel); `alchemy` (🜃 Earth element triangle at the TL of the transmutation circle — green earth pigment); `vitrail` (olive glass pane) |
| Lime | yellow + green at 5/8 : 3/8 | `dark=yellow, light=green, light_density=0.375` | `nightvision` (matched phrase — yellow-biased green stipple, reads as brighter tactical-readout neon glow) |
| Sage | green + white at 1/4 : 3/4 (inverted) | `dark=white, light=green, light_density=0.25` | `nightvision` (scanlines — bbox post-pass flips ~25% of the painted green to white, so scanlines read as ambient ground glow rather than crisp CRT lines) |
| Cream | yellow + white at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=yellow, light=white` | `dispatch` (Layer 0 ground wash); `gothic` (mid-edge border diamonds — candle-flicker warmth); `deco` (rising-sun fan inner rays — 2-tone gradient post-pass flips remaining red to white near the centre dot, simulating a true sunburst's bright core); `astrarium` / `lieder` (Layer 0 ground washes — same recipe reused via `_astrarium_paint_cream_wash`; on `lieder` it is what makes the page read as warm manuscript stock rather than the panel's flat white); `tarot` (reading cartouche via `_tarot_paint_body_panel`, card stock via `_tarot_paint_card_stock`, and the cloth's own base layer in `_tarot_paint_vellum`); `betweenus_dark` (the "Curious" legend dot — the app's pale-gold dark `curious`) |
| Warm off-white | white + yellow at about 15 : 1, scattered | `position_noise(x, y) < 16` picks the yellow pixels: a hash scatter, since an ordered 1-in-4 lattice at this ink pair reads as mustard on the panel | `splitflap` (the wall behind the board) |
| Navy | blue + black at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=blue, light=black` | `bauhaus` (matched phrase — tighter-contrast deeper-blue against the newly-yellow BL corner triangle); `vitrail` (navy "deep glass" pane); `saros` (the cold haze on the horizon left of the sun — a blue channel of at most 24/255 painted into the continuous-tone sky and diffusion-dithered, so it lands as this pair at under a tenth blue) |
| Umber / dust haze (error-diffused) | the haze: white + yellow with a black grain; the plain: red + black with a yellow fleck — no fixed ratio, Floyd–Steinberg against the calibrated inks | `_dither_calibrated` over a continuous-tone scene restricted to black / red / yellow / white | `beksinski` (the whole ground — Beksiński's ochre skies and umber plains; green was tried for the umber, the textbook R+G sepia, and came out of the quantiser as red-and-green confetti on the plain, so the theme admits neither green nor blue) |
| Maroon | red + black at 1/2 : 1/2 | `dark=red, light=black` | `dispatch` (rubber-stamp imprint); `gothic` (corner quatrefoil lobes); `chanbara` (rising-sun disc rim + chop seal) |
| Lavender (3-ink) | red + blue + white at ~1/3 each | (via per-pixel 4×4 Bayer partition: cells 0-4 → red, 5-9 → blue, 10-15 → white) | `lcars` (top + bottom rail blocks — sentinel paint + 3-way Bayer post-pass for canonical Okudagram lavender); `vitrail` (lavender glass pane via `_fill_swatch_stipple_3way`) |
| Plum | red + blue + black at ~1/3 each | (3-ink — via per-pixel 4×4 Bayer partition: cells 0-4 → red, 5-9 → blue, 10-15 → black) | `vitrail` (plum "deep glass" pane via `_fill_swatch_stipple_3way` — the richest jewel in the window) |
| Pallid flesh (3-ink) | white + red + yellow at ~68 : 16 : 16, shading to black | (3-ink — one `BAYER_8x8` rank partitioned four ways via `_bosch_pick`, black taking the lowest ranks for the shadow side) | `bosch` (the Tree-Man's egg-shell body, head and the giant ears — Bosch's nudes are a cool pale flesh, far whiter than the recipe below) |
| Light orange | red + yellow + white at 2/5 : 2/5 : 1/5 | (3-ink — not supported by `draw_text_dithered`; for swatch-rectangle fills use `_fill_swatch_stipple_3way`) | — |
| Out-of-focus brass | black → red → yellow → white ramp, two adjacent inks per pixel | (a blurred luminance field read off `BAYER_8x8`: the integer part picks the pair, the fraction the share; a second blurred alpha field thins it into the ground) | `escritoire` (the inkwell, pen cup and sander beyond the sheet, the pens lying behind, and their reflections on the desk) |
| Mahogany | red + yellow + black, yellow a fixed 30% of the lit share | (two nested stipples on one `BAYER_8x8` read: `tile < d` takes red, `tile < 0.3 d` takes yellow, so the hue holds down a density falloff) | `escritoire` (the desk under the lamp) |

Three-ink mixes (e.g. plum, light orange) need a Bayer partition into three regions rather than two; `draw_text_dithered` only handles the two-ink case. `_fill_swatch_stipple_3way` covers rectangular swatch fills (used by the `diags` theme's reference panel). Add a polygon-aware helper if a future theme needs 3-ink text strokes.

**Designing a new themed accent.**

1. **Pick the target.** Sketch it as a sum of two nearby Spectra 6 inks. The table above covers most named tones; if the target isn't listed, pick the two natives that bracket it on the colour wheel.
2. **Estimate the dominance ratio.** If the two inks have asymmetric luminance (yellow >> red, white >> any chroma, black << any chroma), bias toward the *less* luminous ink so the perceived hue lands on the target rather than on the brighter ink. The deco fix is the canonical example: 50/50 red+yellow looked amber, 5/8 red + 3/8 yellow reads as tangerine.
3. **Choose the pattern** from the patterns table. Start at 50/50 if you don't have a strong prior; move to a 4×4 Bayer biased ratio if 50/50 reads washed-out at panel distance.
4. **Wire it in.** Body text accents: extend `_draw_text_body`'s per-theme switch with another `elif theme == "<name>" and fill == SPECTRA6[<dominant>]: draw_text_dithered(...)`. Decorative graphics: paint the shape in the dominant ink, then do a `BAYER_4x4[y%4][x%4] < threshold and pixels[x, y] == dominant` post-pass to flip the minority pixels to the lighter ink (`draw_deco_border`'s final pass is the reference).
5. **Saturation tier.** Also bump `display_inky.THEME_SATURATION["<theme>"]` to `0.7` if the accent needs to stay punchy against a non-white ground; `0.5` is the gentler tier for solid-red-on-white themes (the `deco` recipe deliberately stays at `0.5` because the red-biased Bayer already corrects the perceived hue without a saturation bump).
6. **Test.** Add a `TestDrawTextDithered`-style ratio assertion if the recipe is novel; add the theme to the renderer golden suite (`tests/test_render_golden.py`) if you want a visual regression fence.
